【澳大利亞聯儲主席稱通脹風險上升 暗示未來或需進一步加息】10月28日，澳大利亞儲備銀行行長米歇爾·布洛克表示，中東衝突推高全球能源價格，人工智慧熱潮及極端天氣也加大部分商品價格上漲壓力，澳大利亞通脹可能高於該行8月預測。澳大利亞通脹近期維持在3.5%至4%，高於2%至3%的目標區間。布洛克稱，國內產能壓力和生產率增長疲弱也加劇通脹，貨幣政策委員會下週將評估此前加息是否足以推動通脹迴歸目標。今年澳聯儲已累計加息100個基點。

### 2026-10-28 09:35:26  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Holds Steady as Fed Decision Looms — Silver held steady above $54 an ounce on Wednesday after gaining some ground in the previous session, as investors awaited the US Federal Reserve’s upcoming policy decision and assessed Israeli election results for potential implications on the Middle East conflict. The Fed is widely expected to keep interest rates unchanged, although traders will look for clues regarding a possible rate hike in December. Meanwhile, the Reserve Bank of New Zealand kept its policy rate steady despite market expectations for another increase, signaling a cautious stance toward further monetary tightening. On the geopolitical front, oil prices remained volatile amid a closely contested Israeli election that could influence the course of the Middle East conflict. Silver has faced sustained pressure this year as regional tensions fueled inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for higher interest rates, reducing the appeal of the non-yielding precious metals.

Kiwi Dollar Tumbles After RBNZ Unexpectedly Holds Rates — The New Zealand dollar fell more than 1% to around $0.559, its lowest level in two weeks, after the Reserve Bank kept interest rates unchanged at 2.75%. This defied market expectations of a 25-basis-point hike. Still, the committee indicated that further monetary policy tightening could be necessary, depending on the economic outlook. The decision comes amid persistent inflationary pressures, with the central bank having raised the cash rate at its two previous meetings over concerns that inflation remained outside its 1-3% target range. Analysts expect price pressures to intensify again in the fourth quarter, potentially pushing inflation above 4% and keeping it elevated for longer than previously anticipated due to elevated energy prices.

【英傑電氣：2026年光伏業務訂單大幅增長 相關收入將在後續週期逐步確認】英傑電氣(300820)10月28日在互動平臺表示，公司營收由多領域電源產品銷售收入共同構成。其中半導體領域銷售收入與訂單大幅增長態勢匹配，前三季度實現同比大幅增長。對比營收結構：2025年第三季度確認了有大額國內光伏業務收入；而2026年第三季度國內光伏板塊收入確認規模相對有限。光伏專案單個合同金額通常較高，儘管半導體業務營收維持高增，但增量尚不足以彌補2026年第三季度國內光伏收入確認形成的缺口。這是造成三季度業績同比下滑的主要原因。現階段國內光伏行業仍處在下行調整週期，致使國內光伏板塊收入確認存在較強的季度波動性。與此同時，公司2026年光伏業務訂單實現大幅增長，訂單絕大部分對應客戶海外專案，目前公司正有序組織生產及交付，相關收入將在後續週期逐步確認，光伏行業仍然有望對公司後續業績形成支撐。

### 2026-10-28 09:18:13  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Aussie Hits 5-Week High on Hot CPI Print — The Australian dollar strengthened to above $0.70, hitting a five-week high after hotter-than-expected inflation data boosted expectations for a fifth interest rate hike this year. Annual inflation jumped to 4.3% in September from 4.0% in August, surpassing the 4.2% forecast and reaching its highest level since March. The pickup pushed inflation further above the RBA’s 2–3% target range, boosting bets on a hike at the November 3 meeting. Monthly CPI rose 0.8%, the biggest increase in six months, also exceeding expectations of 0.7%. The data followed remarks from RBA Governor Michele Bullock earlier on Wednesday, when she warned that upside risks to inflation were materializing and price pressures would likely exceed the central bank’s August forecast. She also cautioned that prolonged deviations from the target could entrench inflation expectations and influence price and wage setting. Traders raised the probability of a November hike to 78%, up from 70% before the inflation report.

【“阿爾納卜”已經在印度沿海登陸 後天西藏降水進入最強時段】這兩天，冷空氣影響北方多地，東北部分地區氣溫將創今年下半年來新低。月底前後還會有冷空氣接連影響我國，北方多地氣溫還會創新低。  明天（29日），受孟加拉灣熱帶系統北上輸送水汽影響，青藏高原部分地區將有較強雨雪天氣。孟加拉灣氣旋風暴“阿爾納卜”目前已經在印度沿海登陸。這個風暴會逐漸北上，強度逐漸減弱。它會帶動偏南氣流向高原輸送暖溼水汽，水汽遇到喜馬拉雅山南麓被迫抬升；與此同時，高空槽東移與風暴環流協同作用，進一步增強水汽輸送與大範圍空氣抬升，這三個因素的疊加影響下，明天夜間到31日，西藏南部會出現雨雪天氣，降水強度具有極端性。明天，西藏西部和南部有中到大雪，局地暴雪、大暴雪。

### 2026-10-28 09:11:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

South Korean Shares Rally on SK Hynix Earnings — The benchmark KOSPI rose 4.70% to above 5,900 on Wednesday, extending gains for another session as revived optimism over artificial intelligence demand and strong earnings from chipmaker SK hynix lifted investor sentiment. The rally tracked overnight gains on Wall Street, where the Nasdaq Composite climbed 1.18%, and the Philadelphia Semiconductor Index jumped 3.6%. SK hynix reported a more than sevenfold increase in third-quarter operating profit to 83.33 trillion won, beating market expectations by over 7%, while announcing an additional 60 trillion won in shareholder returns. Additional upward pressure came from index heavyweights, such as Samsung Electronics (+5.74%), SK Hynix (+3.32%), LG Energy Solution (+2.88%), Hyundai Motor (+2.93%), and KB Financial (+3.39%). Meanwhile, investors remained cautious ahead of the Federal Reserve's interest rate decision early Thursday, seeking clues on the future path of US monetary policy.

【U.S. warns Gulf states it may launch short, intense strike on Iran after midterms】The Wall Street Journal, citing people familiar with the matter, reports the U.S. has asked several Gulf Arab states to prepare for a possible short, intense military strike on Iran after the U.S. midterm elections. Washington has conveyed the warning via diplomatic and military channels and, together with Israel, is reportedly preparing for fresh strikes intended to degrade Tehran—a move that could trigger significant escalation. U.S. forces are deploying a Patriot air‑defense battery from Texas, repositioning troops from vulnerable bases, replenishing munitions, and preparing bombers and fighters; a carrier strike group and a Marine expeditionary unit are also scheduled to join roughly 20 warships already operating in the region next month. Sources say President Trump has not decided but favors a post‑election large‑scale strike that could target Iranian infrastructure and nuclear sites.

【特朗普擬放寬油氣行業甲烷排放管控】美國總統唐納德·特朗普於週二正式提議縮減針對油氣企業的甲烷排放管控措施，這標誌著其政府為撤銷旨在應對人為氣候變化的聯邦法規邁出了最新的重要一步。特朗普在得克薩斯州拉伯克市的一場晚間競選集會上表示：“我將正式廢除拜登政府制定的昂貴且不必要的甲烷排放法規，從而為消費者和能源生產商節省450億美元的成本。”他將這一舉措（目前仍處於提案階段）定位為降低能源成本的手段，以期在下週中期選舉前緩解選民對生活成本的擔憂。該提案旨在修改拜登政府於2024年制定的嚴格標準，呼籲放寬對油氣井、管道及加工設施的甲烷管控。此外，提案還建議廢除旨在幫助減少最大規模甲烷洩漏的所謂“超級排放源計劃”（Super Emitter Program）。特朗普補充稱，該措施一旦最終敲定，將帶來2.1億桶的額外石油產量。

【Australian core inflation accelerates, markets raise odds of RBA rate hike】Australia's trimmed-mean CPI for the three months to September rose 3.7% YoY, the Australian Bureau of Statistics said on Wednesday, above the prior quarter and economists' 3.6% forecast. The policy-sensitive three-year government bond yield climbed as much as 8bps to 4.93%. Traders now price a 78% probability of an RBA rate hike at next Tuesday's policy meeting, up from 70% before the data. ANZ rates strategist Robert Thompson said a hike is now essentially certain given recent RBA comments and worrying report details — core inflation remains too high and market services prices have risen again after stripping volatile items — and he would be very surprised if the RBA did not raise rates.

Japanese Shares Follow Wall Street Higher — The Nikkei 225 Index climbed 1.1% toward 64,000 on Wednesday, rising for the second straight session and tracking Wall Street higher amid renewed optimism surrounding artificial intelligence. The rally followed Broadcom’s announcement of $63 billion in financing linked to Anthropic’s use of its technology. In South Korea, chipmaker SK Hynix also reported a significant jump in revenue and operating profit, driven by robust AI demand. Meanwhile, oil prices declined amid renewed diplomatic efforts to resolve the Middle East conflict, easing cost pressures on oil-importing economies. Investors also turned their attention to major central bank decisions due this week, with the Fed, ECB, and BOJ widely expected to keep interest rates unchanged, while markets assess the possibility of rate hikes in December. Technology and AI-related shares led the gains, with strong gains from Kioxia (7.3%), Advantest (2.3%), Taiyo Yuden (7.7%), SoftBank Group (2.3%) and Ibiden Co (5.6%).

【花旗和Jefferies下調泡泡瑪特目標價 公司三季度業績不及預期】花旗稱泡泡瑪特三季度收入下滑45%-50%不及該行預期，主要因中國和亞洲的銷售低於預期。考慮到營收減弱和利潤率壓力，該行下調對該公司的盈利預測並將目標價調低至162港元。Jefferies也將泡泡瑪特目標價下調9.6%至132港元。花旗分析師Lydia Ling等在報告中指出，泡泡瑪特美國和歐洲的銷售額大致符合預期。在三季度受去年同期高基數影響後，預計公司四季度業績將環比改善。預計公司今年收入將同比下滑16%。Jefferies分析師JingJue Pei等在報告中指出，由於海外業務經營去槓桿，將公司2027-2028年的盈利預測下調9%-12%。儘管公司三季度業績疲軟，但當前風險回報已趨於均衡。泡泡瑪特今年來下跌近30%，最新收盤價為132港元。

【華泰證券：本輪厄爾尼諾仍在增強，關注供需兌現與行業分化】華泰證券研報指出，本輪厄爾尼諾仍在增強，產業影響或延續至2027年，投資機會取決於供需變化能否轉化為企業利潤。按ONI口徑，2026年7—9月指數已達+2.2℃，本輪觀測強度已處歷史高位；同期RONI為+1.7℃，CPC於10月預測10~12月RONI中位數將升至+2.74℃，年末附近仍是主要峰值視窗。歷史經驗表明，氣候強度與資產漲幅缺乏穩定對應關係，核心產區受損程度、庫存緩衝及前期預期計入程度，才是判斷行情持續性的關鍵。本輪重點關注東南亞熱帶作物供給、高溫電力需求，以及2027年我國南方可能出現的異常降水及其產業影響。

### 2026-10-28 08:24:30  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #XAUUSD #Impact bullish #S&P500 #CSI300

【CITIC Securities: Fed policy likely driven more by politics than economics】CITIC Securities says rapid AI-led growth has lifted headline US activity but masks sectoral divergence: production and credit are overheated while employment and household demand remain soft. The firm judges Fed policy is more likely to be swayed by political forces than by fundamentals; some Fed officials may coordinate with the Treasury to compress long-term yields. Post-midterms, reduced uncertainty could be a short-term positive for risk assets, though increased political polarization could create fresh shocks. Asset scenarios: base-case “hot continuation” — US equities, gold and China A-shares outperform, bonds lag; alternative “cold diffusion” — US Treasuries, gold and A-shares lead. Gold is bullish across scenarios.

【中信建投：無布是高速銅互聯升級關鍵一步 優先關注CCL/PP龍頭】中信建投研報稱，無布是高速銅互聯升級的關鍵一步，但並非將整塊PCB的玻纖布全部去除，而是依據效能與可靠性約束，在特定介質層實施少布/無布。無布應用分為算力PCB與光模組兩種場景，共同目標是降低損耗、提升訊號完整性，差異在切入位置與材料形態。無布推動產業鏈價值向CCL/PP環節集中，優先關注CCL/PP龍頭，同時重視PCB加工及上游材料增量機會。高速銅互聯向少布/無布升級，CCL/PP有望成為核心受益環節。同時，PCB加工難度提升及樹脂、球矽等上游材料需求增長有望帶來新增機會。

### 2026-10-28 08:07:49  #公司 #市場

【SK海力士季度業績超預期 宣佈規模100萬億韓元的2026年股東回報計劃】SK海力士第三季度利潤超預期激增六倍，並宣佈規模達100萬億韓元（750億美元）的2026年股東回報計劃，凸視訊記憶體儲晶片需求依然強勁。這家全球第二大儲存晶片製造商公佈，截至9月的季度營業利潤為83.3萬億韓元，高於分析師平均預估的77.5萬億韓元。營收升至104.6萬億韓元，也超過99萬億韓元的平均預測。這份業績增強了市場對AI資料中心建設持續拉動儲存晶片需求、進而支撐公司盈利的信心。近幾周，隨著大型AI基礎設施專案面臨融資和建設挑戰，AI投資熱潮正受到越來越多的質疑。SK海力士向英偉達供應高頻寬儲存晶片(HBM)，已成為AI熱潮中最大的受益者之一。儲存晶片供不應求導致價格飆升，該公司營業利潤率處於歷史高位。SK海力士週三表示，預計儲存晶片供應短缺將持續至2030年。麥格理的研究顯示，儲存晶片目前約佔AI資料中心全部支出的三分之一。SK海力士憑藉與英偉達的緊密合作，在這一市場佔據重要地位。麥格理表示，隨著HBM價格預計在2027年上漲逾一倍，未來幾年儲存晶片在整體AI硬體支出中的佔比還將上升。SK海力士第三季度股息為每股2萬韓元，計劃2026年至少派發30萬億韓元現金股息，並至少回購和登出60萬億韓元股票。

### 2026-10-28 08:02:41  #MKT News #Market Themes #Chip Wars #AI Revolution #000660.KS #Impact bullish

【SK Hynix announces KRW100 trillion shareholder return plan; says memory supply shortage to persist to 2030】SK Hynix reported quarterly operating profit of KRW83.3 trillion for the quarter to September (consensus KRW76.7 trillion) and revenue of KRW104.6 trillion (consensus KRW98.3 trillion), both single-quarter records; quarterly profit rose more than sevenfold YoY. The company said it will implement an additional KRW100 trillion (~$75bn) shareholder return program in 2026. Operating margins remain at elevated, historic levels amid tight supply. SK Hynix expects memory-chip supply shortages to persist through 2030, citing rising technical difficulty at advanced process nodes and long lead times for new fabs that constrain near-term supply expansion. It sees near-term demand driven by enterprise and consumer AI adoption and mid-to-long-term growth from physical-AI applications such as autonomous driving and robotics.

【SK海力士預計第四季度HBM4出貨量和營收將超過HBM3E】10月28日，SK海力士表示，公司將快速提高第六代10奈米級（1c）工藝的比重，並增加DDR5和麵向AI伺服器的LPDDR5 SOCAMM的銷售，進一步強化DRAM以高附加值產品為中心的產品組合。為應對激增的HBM需求，公司計劃年底前將M15X工廠的產能提升至最大水平，以擴大HBM4生產。據此，公司預計第四季度HBM4將在出貨量和營業收入方面均超越HBM3E，確立其主力產品地位。在NAND快閃記憶體方面，公司計劃第四季度將321層工藝的比重提升至韓國本土產能的50%以上，以此增加高效能、大容量eSSD的供應。中長期來看，公司計劃以高頻寬快閃記憶體和系統最佳化技術為基礎，將NAND快閃記憶體打造為提升AI服務吞吐量與經濟性的核心解決方案。

【公募基金三季報披露完畢 債券基金規模創歷史新高】10月27日，公募基金2026年三季報披露完畢。公募基金最新前十大重倉股出爐，分別是中際旭創、新易盛、藥明康德、長鑫科技、源傑科技、東山精密、寧德時代、寒武紀、北方華創、滬電股份。與2026年二季度末相比，藥明康德、長鑫科技、滬電股份三隻個股新進前十大重倉股。其中，藥明康德成為公募基金增持市值最多的個股。從基金規模來看，截至三季度末，債券型基金總規模超過12.6萬億元，創歷史新高。相較於二季度末，債基規模增加超5000億元，獲淨申購超過2800億份，成為股票型基金、混合型基金、債券型基金、貨幣型基金、商品型基金、QDII、FOF等七大類基金中，獲淨申購最多的一類基金產品。三季度主動權益基金（包括普通股票型、偏股混合型、平衡混合型、靈活配置型基金）規模合計減少超9000億元，規模超200億元的主動權益產品數量減少至5只。然而，三季度仍有東方人工智慧主題、銀華積體電路、財通成長優選、信澳業績驅動、富國新興產業等一批科技風格基金獲資金增持。（中國證券報）

【國開行進出口銀行完成新型政策性金融工具投放】10月27日，國家開發銀行、中國進出口銀行分別宣佈完成2026年新型政策性金融工具投放任務，合計投放4850億元，資金全部用於補充專案資本金。國開行投放3650億元，涉及專案總投資5.5萬億元，其中向民間投資和民間資本參與專案投放1279.9億元，佔比35.1%；對人工智慧、數字經濟、綠色低碳、生物醫藥、消費5個領域專案投放1790.1億元，佔比49%。進出口銀行投放1200億元，支援專案超500個，拉動總投資逾1.7萬億元，側重民間投資撬動、新興產業投放、重點區域引領，推動210餘個民間投資及民間資本參與專案落地，放款金額佔比近四成；220餘個數字經濟和人工智慧等新興產業領域專案放款金額佔比近五成；11個經濟體量較大省份獲得傾斜支援，推動330餘個專案落地，放款金額佔比超七成。今年政府工作報告提出發行新型政策性金融工具8000億元，帶動更多社會資本參與投資。（證券時報）

Brent Holds Losses Amid Israeli Elections — Brent crude held below $114 per barrel on Wednesday after sliding for two straight sessions, as markets monitored election results in Israel that could change the trajectory of the Middle East conflict. Exit polls indicated that opposition leader Gadi Eisenkot held a narrow lead over Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu, although neither bloc secured a majority and vote counting was still underway. The results point to weakening support for Netanyahu, a dominant figure in Israeli politics for decades who has guided the country through nearly three years of continuous conflict. Meanwhile, markets assessed renewed diplomatic efforts in the region after officials from Saudi Arabia, Pakistan, and Turkey met with Iranian leaders on Monday. Iran’s Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei also called for an end to the current state of “neither war nor peace.” In the US, crude inventories dropped by 2 million barrels in the week ending October 23, signaling tighter oil supplies.

【年內117家公司港股IPO募資逾3900億港元】港股IPO（首次公開募股）發行仍保持高熱度。10月28日，蘇州東山精密製造股份有限公司正式登陸港股市場。萬得資料顯示，按上市日期計算，截至10月27日，今年以來，港股市場有117只新股IPO，同比增長53.95%；募集資金規模3915.16億港元，同比增長98.93%。與去年同期相比，發行節奏、融資體量同步提升，硬體裝置、半導體、軟體服務等企業成為主角。德勤中國將港股全年IPO募資預期上調至不少於4800億港元，四季度大型專案能否如期落地，是觀察全年能否衝擊歷史紀錄的關鍵視窗。（證券日報）

### 2026-10-28 06:51:06  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Extends Losses Ahead of RBNZ, Fed Decisions — The NZX 50 dropped 83 points, or 0.6%, to 13,597 in Wednesday morning deals, extending losses for a fourth straight session as traders remained cautious ahead of the RBNZ’s and the Fed’s monetary policy decisions later today. The market expects the RBNZ to raise the official cash rate by 25 bps to 3% for a third straight meeting, its highest level since September 2025, amid elevated inflation driven by higher oil prices. Although annual inflation slowed to 3.7% in Q3, it remained above the RBNZ’s 1–3% target range. However, moderating oil prices limited the decline, and an upbeat overnight session on Wall Street helped limit losses, supported by a tech rebound ahead of major companies' earnings reports. The broader index touched its lowest level since September 15, dragged down mainly by healthcare, utilities, and energy stocks, with notable losses in Fisher & Paykel (-2.5%), Mercury NZ (-1.5%), Meridian Energy (-1.2%), Contact Energy (-0.8%), and Mainfreight (-0.5%).

【中期分紅陸續兌現，銀行股紅利屬性持續強化】銀行中期分紅正從方案披露進入密集實施階段。上海銀行近日釋出2026年半年度權益分派實施公告，共計派發現金紅利超40億元。與此同時，蘇農銀行、工商銀行、農業銀行等銀行均在10月落地中期分紅。六家國有大行更是將2026年中期分紅比例從30%提升至31%。業內人士表示，銀行基本面改善為銀行分紅提供了空間。在低利率環境下，高股息銀行股配置價值凸顯，銀行也更有動力透過中期分紅穩定投資者預期、增強對長期資金的吸引力。（中證報）

Insiders told Gurman Apple has discussed pricing a new 14‑inch with the M5 Pro chip at $2,899, about $400 above the current M5 Pro 14‑inch. The existing non‑touch 16‑inch MacBook Pro starts at $2,999; the new 16‑inch would start at least $3,399. 【FT: US, Russia discuss post-war return of US firms to Russian energy sector】FT reports US and Russia have discussed economic incentives to advance peace talks that would permit US firms to re-enter Russia’s energy sector. Proposals cited include restoring Sakhalin oil and gas JVs, resuming Russian oil exports to Europe via the Druzhba pipeline, partial restoration of Nord Stream gas flows, Novatek participation in Alaska LNG, sale of Lukoil’s overseas assets to investors close to the White House, easing potash sanctions, and transferring management of the ZAPORIZHZHIA NUCLEAR PLANT to a US entity. US officials say any cooperation must be part of a comprehensive peace framework and no agreement has been reached. The FT adds Europe opposes sanction relief before the conflict ends and US companies are reportedly cautious, leaving implementation highly uncertain.

Brent Drops on Middle East Diplomacy Hopes — Brent crude fell to around $114 a barrel on Tuesday, as renewed diplomatic efforts raised hopes of de-escalating tensions in the Middle East. Iran’s Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei called for an end to the current state of “neither war nor peace,” signaling a possible opening for negotiations. Officials from Saudi Arabia, Pakistan, and Turkey met in Tehran on Monday and agreed to continue discussions, while Russian President Vladimir Putin was expected to meet Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian and Qatar’s emir in Moscow. These developments improved prospects for de-escalation, although significant risks remain. Meanwhile, US crude inventories fell by 2.031 million barrels in the week ended October 23rd, defying expectations for an increase of 800,000 barrels and extending the previous week’s 4.93 million-barrel draw, adding to supply concerns. Houthi attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure and Iranian strikes on tankers in the Strait of Hormuz continued to support prices.

### 2026-10-28 05:12:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Chile Holds Key Interest Rate Amid External Risks — Chile’s central bank kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 4.5% at its October 2026 meeting, citing geopolitical tensions, tighter international financial conditions, and rising global inflation risks. The Board noted that escalating tensions in the Middle East had worsened the outlook for energy prices. Meanwhile, an unusually intense El Niño phenomenon had begun to affect international food prices, adding to cost pressures from energy, transportation, and fertilizers associated with armed conflicts. Copper prices, however, remained near the levels recorded at the previous meeting. Against this backdrop, concerns about global inflation continued to mount. Policymakers also highlighted tighter international financial conditions amid expectations of a more restrictive monetary policy stance, falling equity markets, a stronger US dollar, and rising long-term interest rates. The Board left the future path of monetary policy open.

US Crude Inventories Fall Unexpectedly — US crude oil inventories fell by 2.031 million barrels in the week ended October 23rd, compared with market expectations for an increase of 800,000 barrels. In the previous week, crude stocks declined by 4.93 million barrels. Inventories at Cushing fell by 453,000 barrels after rising by 900,000 barrels a week earlier. In contrast, gasoline inventories increased by 2.39 million barrels, following a 2 million-barrel decline in the previous week. Distillate stocks rose by 380,000 barrels, partially reversing the previous week's 2.6 million-barrel draw. Meanwhile, US oil production fell to 13.269 million barrels per day in the week ended October 16th, down from 13.679 million barrels per day a week earlier.

Ibovespa Extends Post-Election Losses — The Ibovespa fell 0.5% to close at 175,710, extending losses after plunging 13.7% on Monday following President Lula’s re-election. Lula’s victory erased the reduction in the risk premium that followed the right-wing landslide in the first round. Investors had widely viewed a potential Bolsonaro government as more likely to rein in public spending, seen as essential to lowering interest rates. Major banks fell, with Itaú down 1.2% and Bradesco losing 1.4%. Santander shed 2.2% ahead of its earnings report on Wednesday. Petrobras dropped another 2% as oil prices declined, following an 8.2% plunge on Monday. Vale fell 2.1% ahead of its Thursday earnings report, after outperforming the index during Monday’s rout. Gerdau shed 2.7% despite strong results. WEG extended gains by 3.7% after reporting upbeat earnings last week. Ambev added 1.3% ahead of its results on Wednesday.

TSX Rises Ahead of BoC and Fed Meetings — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose 0.6% to close at 36,171 ahead of monetary policy decisions from the Bank of Canada and the US Federal Reserve later this week. The BoC meets Wednesday, with markets pricing in a 25-basis-point hike, while the Fed is widely expected to hold rates steady. Canadian bond yields fell as the oil rally paused, easing energy-driven inflation concerns and supporting bank stocks. BMO gained 1.2%, Scotiabank rose 0.8%, and CIBC added 1.1%. Mining stocks advanced as gold prices picked up, with Agnico Eagle rising 2.9% ahead of its earnings report on Wednesday, Barrick gaining 2.8%, and Wheaton Precious Metals climbing 3.1%. Meanwhile, the US and Canada are reportedly negotiating a deal to ease US tariffs on Canadian steel and autos. BRP rose 2.7%, while Teck Resources gained 3.5%. 【China urges parties to advance US-Iran ceasefire and talks】China's UN ambassador Fu Cong told a Security Council open meeting on the Iran nuclear file on 27 that parties should push for a US-Iran ceasefire and talks. Fu blamed the US's unilateral withdrawal from the JCPOA and the reinstatement and escalation of unilateral sanctions and 'maximum pressure' for the current impasse. He said the JCPOA and UNSC Resolution 2231 demonstrate political-diplomatic means are the only way to resolve the issue, and that the agreement's core objectives and principles — including step-by-step reciprocity and balanced rights and obligations — must be preserved. Fu added Iran should honour its commitment not to develop nuclear weapons and that states must respect Iran's NPT-guaranteed right to peaceful nuclear energy.

【Exit polls show no clear winner in Israeli parliamentary vote】Voting in Israel's 26th Knesset election ended at 04:00 Beijing time on Oct. 28. The Central Elections Committee said turnout had reached 74.3% by 20:00 that day, versus 66.3% at the same point in 2022 — the highest for that time slot since 1988; final turnout is expected to rise. Parties must pass a 3.25% threshold to enter the 120-seat Knesset. Exit polls from Channel 12 and Channel 11 project Prime Minister NETANYAHU's Likud as the largest party with 24–25 seats, and the list led by former IDF chief of staff Gadi Eizenkot on 21–22 seats.

Wall Street Rises as Tech Stocks Rebound — US stock indices closed higher on Tuesday as technology stocks rebounded ahead of earnings from major industry players. 【以色列議會選舉投票結束】當地時間10月27日晚10點（北京時間10月28日凌晨4點），以色列第26屆議會選舉投票結束。根據以色列中央選舉委員會公佈的資料，截至當天晚間8時，選舉的投票率已達到74.3%，高於2022年大選同一時段的66.3%。中央選舉委員會表示，這是自1988年以來，該時段錄得的全國最高投票率。預計最終投票率將繼續升高。 根據以色列選舉法，政黨須跨過3.25%的得票門檻方可進入議會，並按比例分配120個席位。初步計票結果預計10月28日或29日公佈，最終結果由中央選舉委員會於11月4日正式公佈。此後，中央選舉委員會向總統赫爾佐格提交最終結果，赫爾佐格將在一週內與各黨議員代表協商，並授權最有可能成功組閣的議員組建政府。獲授權議員擁有28天組閣期，可申請延長14天；若失敗，總統可授權另一名議員，期限28天且不得延期；仍失敗則由61名議員聯名請求授權第三名候選人，期限14天，否則議會解散並舉行新一輪選舉。（CCTV國際時訊）

### 2026-10-28 02:40:48  #國際

【普京與卡達埃米爾舉行會晤 討論中東局勢】據克里姆林宮網站10月27日訊息，俄羅斯總統普京與卡達埃米爾（國家元首）塔米姆當天在莫斯科“天然氣出口國論壇”峰會期間舉行會晤。普京指出，卡達在調解各種危機局勢、包括波斯灣局勢中發揮著重要作用，俄卡在國際舞臺上的協調有所加強。卡達和巴基斯坦斡旋下籤署的美伊諒解備忘錄作為推進談判程序的基礎仍然有效。俄方從伊朗衝突一開始就主張停止軍事行動和尋求政治外交解決，並準備採取一切力所能及的措施幫助解決問題。塔米姆表示，當前中東正經歷困難時期，美伊緊張局勢對整個地區產生負面影響和後果，包括霍爾木茲海峽危機和能源領域危機。卡達珍視並期待俄方調解霍爾木茲海峽局勢和地區其他危機，包括巴勒斯坦問題以及加沙的人道主義問題。經濟合作方面，普京表示，俄羅斯和卡達都是天然氣生產大國，也都是出口大國，全球能源市場穩定很大程度上取決於兩國合作與協調。塔米姆表示，過去十年兩國關係在經濟和投資領域積極發展，卡達對俄投資規模顯著。（央視新聞）

【梅拉尼婭：俄烏奧列什基停火提前生效 範圍已擴大】美國總統特朗普夫人梅拉尼婭27日在社交媒體發文稱，俄烏在赫爾松地區奧列什基的停火協議當天提前生效，比原計劃提前兩天，範圍已擴大至奧列什基以外區域，以便人道主義救援車隊通行；俄軍總參謀長格拉西莫夫已證實。俄方和烏方高階官員均證實雙方當天在奧列什基停火以開展人道主義行動。白宮23日宣告稱，梅拉尼婭促成該停火協議，停火從10月29日開始，範圍僅限於奧列什基市，俄烏同意暫停一切軍事行動，安排平民透過指定安全通道撤離；衝突爆發以來奧列什基大部分居民已離開，現估計仍有1700人至2000人留守，主要是無法離開的老年人，另有約50名兒童。（新華社）

### 2026-10-28 02:25:14  #公司 #國際

【歐盟委員會提出加強歐盟氣候風險應對能力】歐盟委員會27日釋出歐盟氣候韌性框架，旨在加強歐盟預判、應對和管理氣候風險的能力。歐委會當天釋出公告說，今年夏季歐洲遭遇持續的熱浪和乾旱天氣。近年來，歐洲還多次遭遇嚴重洪災，氣候變化對民眾、企業和基礎設施的影響日益凸顯。根據估算，2022年至2025年間，氣候災害造成的經濟損失約為2000億歐元。歐盟氣候韌性框架提出加強氣候風險預判和應對，將氣候韌性納入歐盟主要政策領域，併為地方和區域政府提供適應氣候變化的支援。歐委會預計，歐洲每年需要約700億歐元的投資，才能使基礎設施和經濟適應未來的氣候條件。（央視新聞）

【Refinitiv: Russia Baltic, Black Sea crude exports seen down 100,000-200,000 bpd in November】Refinitiv, citing traders, expects crude exports from Russia’s Baltic and Black Sea ports to fall 100,000–200,000 bpd MoM in November. Traders attribute the decline to refineries exiting maintenance and raising runs to increase diesel output for a Russia‑US diesel supply agreement reached on Oct 9; Russia has partly restored diesel export quotas and the US has eased fuel-export sanctions. Risks remain: Ukrainian strikes on energy infrastructure, roughly three-week loading delays at Novorossiysk, and falling Russian crude production that may limit further refinery throughput, leaving November export plans subject to change.

FTSE 100 Falls as Corporate Results Disappoint — The FTSE 100 fell more than 0.5% on Tuesday as disappointing corporate results and weaker energy stocks weighed on the index. HSBC declined 5.4% after reporting third-quarter profits below expectations, while Reckitt dropped 4.9% after cutting its sales outlook amid supply constraints in the US and weaker vitamin sales in China. Oil majors also came under pressure, with Shell losing 1.7% and BP falling 2.8% as Brent crude prices retreated on renewed hopes of a diplomatic resolution to the US-Iran conflict. Mining stocks provided some support, however, as Antofagasta climbed 4.6%, while Endeavour Mining, Fresnillo and Rio Tinto gained around 2.5% each. Anglo American also advanced 2.3%, benefiting from strength across the sector. Meanwhile, UK shop price inflation accelerated to 2.1% year-on-year in October, up from 1.4% in September and above economists’ expectations of 1.6%.

### 2026-10-28 00:50:23  #公司 #市場 #央行 #國際

【AI融資焦慮或近頂峰 億萬富豪Novogratz預計信貸市場將趨穩】加密金融服務和人工智慧資料中心運營商Galaxy Digital Inc.的億萬富豪創始人兼執行長Michael Novogratz表示，全球信貸市場對AI支出的焦慮可能已接近緩解的轉折點。Galaxy公佈第三財季業績後，Novogratz週二在電話會議上表示，“信貸市場目前有些‘消化不良’。市場的緊張情緒可能已接近頂峰。走出困境並沒有一條輕鬆的路，但我認為時間會讓情況逐漸好轉。”Novogratz認為，Meta Platforms Inc.進行股權融資以及Anthropic PBC計劃首次公開募股，都是壓力可能緩解的跡象，“可以這樣理解：更多股權融資，更少信貸融資。從某種程度上說，過去一週對信貸市場是件好事，因為我們看到了如此大規模的股權融資。”Novogratz認為，伊朗戰爭可能結束也是認為信貸市場趨穩的另一個原因，因為這可能推動油價下跌，從而減輕美聯儲為遏制通脹而加息的壓力。

### 2026-10-28 00:43:01  #MKT News

【Report: Russian refinery crude runs rise 25% in late October after Russia-Ukraine energy ceasefire】Refinitiv, citing three industry sources, said Russian refinery crude runs recovered rapidly after drone attacks stopped affecting plants on Oct. 16, rising to about 5.0 mln b/d in the second half of October, roughly 25% above the start of the month. Diesel production increased by at least 50,000 tonnes/day to about 225,000 tonnes/day, with roughly 80,000 tonnes/day available for export. Ukrainian drone strikes since mid-March had caused domestic fuel shortages, prompted temporary bans on gasoline, jet fuel and diesel exports and forced imports of refined products. After the US pushed talks and helped arrange a diesel supply deal, Trump announced on Oct. 11 that Russia and Ukraine had agreed an energy ceasefire. Deputy Prime Minister Novak said refineries are rapidly restarting idled units and that needed oil-and-gas equipment can largely be produced domestically.

Arabica Coffee Futures at Over 2-Week Low — Arabica coffee futures traded around $2.9 per pound, their lowest since October 9, amid improving supply prospects and continued selling pressure linked to the Brazilian real. The currency has recovered some ground since its sharp post-election decline following Lula’s surprise re-election. but remained below pre-election levels. Meanwhile, attention remains focused on the development of Brazil’s next crop and the replenishment of global stocks. Recent rainfall has supported flowering and early crop development, improving production prospects, although concerns over irregular precipitation and high temperatures persist. Export figures have also pointed to stronger supply flows, with Brazilian coffee shipments reaching approximately 3.30 million bags by October 22, up 43.2% from the same period a year earlier, according to Cecafé. Certified Arabica stocks remain historically low, which recently raised concerns about deliveries of the nearest contracts.

【日本政府開出標準 大阪等四地爭當“副首都”】日本政府27日批准一項政令，列明首都東京以外行政區成為指定“副首都”需滿足的各項條件。所謂“副首都”可在東京遭受大地震等重大災難時承擔部分首都功能，也可解決東京首都職能過於集中的問題。根據日本內閣會議27日批准的政令，各道府縣想成為指定“副首都”，必須滿足多項條件，包括經濟規模佔日本國內生產總值(GDP)2.1%以上、轄區內至少有一座城市人口占全國人口1.2%以上、所處地理位置可避開東京大地震或富士山爆發等嚴重災害影響，另外還必須按規定重新構建行政體制。按照已公佈的標準，北海道、愛知縣、大阪府、福岡縣都可能有資格成為指定“副首都”，四地都計劃爭取獲得這一資格。日本內閣官房長官木原稔27日在記者會上說，若這四地均提出申請且符合條件，則四地都會被指定為“副首都”。（央視新聞）

Cotton Drops Toward Three-Month Low — Cotton futures fell below 80 cents per pound, approaching a three-month low as rising harvest supplies weighed on prices. The US harvest continues to advance, with 52% of the crop havested by the week ending October 25th, above the five-year average of 45%. Peak harvest typically pressures prices as newly available supplies enter the market. Seasonal supply pressure is compounded by intensifying export competition with Brazil, the world’s leading cotton exporter, which is expected to ship around 3.4 million tonnes, according to Anea, compared with the USDA’s US export forecast of 2.69 million tonnes. Meanwhile, progress in US–China trade talks has yet to deliver clear tariff relief, prompting Chinese buyers to scale back foreign purchases, reflected in a drop in China’s cotton imports of 42.6% year-on-year in September. In contrast, India is expected to extend duty-free cotton imports through December amid tight domestic supplies, supporting some demand.

Brazil’s 10-Year Yield Retreats After National Treasury Intervention — Brazil’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 14.97% after reaching as high as 15.6% on October 26th, following President Lula’s runoff victory over Flávio Bolsonaro, as the National Treasury intervened to ease market pressures. The Treasury announced it would suspend its regular Selic-linked bonds auction and the buyback operation for inflation-linked bonds to address market dysfunction. Investors remain focused on fiscal policy, particularly the government’s commitment to controlling spending and public debt, which has risen significantly in recent years. The appointment of the next finance minister will be a key signal. Markets had viewed a Bolsonaro administration as more likely to curb public spending, seen as essential to lowering interest rates. 【房貸利率飆升之際 美國新建住宅銷量下降】美國9月新建住宅銷量小幅下降，建築商努力降價並提供促銷優惠，未能抵消抵押貸款利率飆升的影響。美國政府週二公佈的資料顯示，9月新建單戶型住宅銷量下降0.6%，折年率為67.6萬套。這高於彭博調查經濟學家的預期中值65.1萬套。新建住宅銷售價格中值同比下降3.9%，至400,400美元，今年前九個月中已有七個月同比下跌。9月低迷的銷售資料凸顯美國住房市場面臨的嚴峻挑戰。抵押貸款利率上月升約0.5個百分點，並在10月初升至近三年來最高水平，此後僅小幅回落。借貸成本飆升削弱了潛在購房者的需求，並推動一項住宅建築商信心指標降至14年來最低水平。週二公佈的資料顯示，9月新屋待售庫存同比下降2.3%，至47.6萬套。按當前銷售速度計算，相當於8.4個月的供應量，為今年以來最低，但仍遠高於疫情前水平。

Ibovespa Reverses Early Gains — The Ibovespa edged below 176,000 on Tuesday, erasing earlier gains after plunging 13.7% on Monday following President Lula’s re-election. The index had initially risen in a corrective move after a wave of selling in Brazilian assets in the previous session. Lula’s victory erased the risk-premium reduction that followed the right-wing landslide in the first round. The selloff largely reflects market disappointment over expectations of a faster fiscal adjustment under the next administration. Investors had widely viewed a potential Bolsonaro government as more likely to rein in public spending, seen as essential to lowering interest rates. Financial stocks reversed early gains, with Itaú and Bradesco each falling 1%, extending losses of around 16% on Monday. Petrobras dropped another 1% as oil prices declined, following an 8.2% plunge in the previous session. Vale fell more than 1.5% ahead of its earnings report due Thursday, after outperforming the index on Monday.

【全國人大常委會副委員長鄭建邦率團出席第十二屆金磚國家議會論壇】
26日至27日，全國人大常委會副委員長鄭建邦率團出席在印度新德里召開的第十二屆金磚國家議會論壇並作主旨發言。
鄭建邦表示，中國國家主席習近平在金磚國家領導人第十八次會晤中提出，金磚國家要做促進創新發展的先鋒、維護和平穩定的先鋒、推動文明互鑑的先鋒、改革完善全球治理的先鋒，這為金磚合作明確歷史使命、指出前進方向。金磚國家已經成為名副其實的全球南方第一方陣，金磚國家議會合作作為金磚合作的重要組成部分，是夯實金磚合作根基、增強制度韌性、破解治理困難、守護共同未來的關鍵力量。金磚國家議會要加強團結合作，勇做時代先鋒，引領國際秩序朝著更加公正合理的方向發展。
會議期間，鄭建邦分別會見了印度、俄羅斯、泰國、烏干達、白俄羅斯、古巴、伊朗等國議會領導人，就深化立法機構合作等交換了意見。（新華社）

Dallas Fed Service-Sector Activity Index Rebounds in October — The general business activity index for the service sector, published by the Federal Reserve Bank of Dallas, rose to 4.0 in October 2026 from -1.8 in September. The revenue index increased five points to 4.4. Labor market measures pointed to continued employment growth and longer workweeks. The employment and part-time employment indexes were little changed at 2.8 and 2.6, respectively, while the hours worked index edged up to 2.2 from 0.1. The company outlook index rose to 3.0 from -3.2, while the outlook uncertainty index fell five points to 16.3. Price and wage pressures remained elevated, with the input prices index little changed at 41.7, well above its series average of 28.0. The selling prices index edged up three points to 18.2. Expectations for future activity improved, with the future revenue index rising five points to 43.6 and the future general business activity index climbing to 19.6 from 10.7.

Lumber Hits Lowest Level in Over 2 Years — Lumber futures fell below $515 per thousand board feet to reach its lowest level since September 2024, amid persistent weakness in the U.S. housing market and elevated input costs. The White House removed 50% tariffs on Canadian plywood, effective October 30th, though softwood lumber tariffs remained in place and elevated, potentially making plywood a relatively cheaper substitute in certain construction applications. While the Commerce Department lowered antidumping and countervailing duties on Canadian softwood to 24.75% from 35.16%, a separate 10% US tariff brings total duties to 34.75%. The US Lumber Coalition backed the decision, as forestry groups have long accused Canada of dumping lumber in the US. President Trump also signed an order aimed at reducing homeownership costs. The measures come as the U.S. housing sector remains under pressure from high mortgage rates and construction costs, with homebuilder confidence falling to its lowest level in 14 years.

【美國耐用品訂單創五個月最大增幅 商用飛機訂單大增】美國9月耐用品訂單創五個月來最大增幅，飛機和其他企業裝置訂單走強，進一步顯示製造業的增長動能。美國商務部週二公佈的資料顯示，使用壽命至少三年的耐用品訂單金額增長1.8%，前一個月的資料上修為增長0.1%。核心資本品訂單9月增長0.5%。耐用品報告顯示，商用飛機訂單增長28.4%。波音9月報告的訂單數量高於前一個月。電腦、機械和汽車訂單也出現增加。在人工智慧(AI)相關支出帶動下，今年企業投資表現強勁。耐用品報告顯示，這一趨勢延續至9月，這意味著週四公佈的第三季度國內生產總值(GDP)初值可能顯示資本支出表現強勁。“9月耐用品報告顯示企業投資具有韌性，”BMO Capital Markets高階經濟學家Priscilla Thiagamoorthy在報告中表示，“飛機訂單推動整體資料上升，但基礎需求繼續擴張，而資本品出貨走強表明，裝置支出在第三季度末對經濟增長起到支撐作用，並在進入第四季度之際保持強勁動能。”

Richmond Fed Services Index Improves Slightly in October — The Richmond Fed’s Fifth District Service Sector Survey edged up in October 2026, with the revenues index rising to 2 from zero in September and the demand index increasing to 7 from 2. Expectations remained positive, with future revenues and demand indexes firmly in expansionary territory. The local business conditions index improved to -5 from -8, while the future local business conditions index was unchanged. The current employment index rose slightly to 3 from 2, while the future employment index fell to 11 from 19. Both current and future wage indexes remained positive. 【日本政府開出標準 大阪等四地爭當“副首都” 】日本政府27日批准一項政令，列明首都東京以外行政區成為指定“副首都”需滿足的各項條件。所謂“副首都”可在東京遭受大地震等重大災難時承擔部分首都功能，也可解決東京首都職能過於集中的問題。根據日本內閣會議27日批准的政令，各道府縣想成為指定“副首都”，必須滿足多項條件，包括經濟規模佔日本國內生產總值(GDP)2.1%以上、轄區內至少有一座城市人口占全國人口1.2%以上、所處地理位置可避開東京大地震或富士山爆發等嚴重災害影響，另外還必須按規定重新構建行政體制。（新華社）

US New Home Sales Slightly Down in September — New single-family home sales in the US fell 0.6% month-on-month to a seasonally adjusted annual rate of 676,000 in September 2026, down from a revised eight-month high of 680,000 in August but comfortably above market expectations of 650,000. The data point to continued housing market challenges as elevated mortgage rates and rising inflation weigh on affordability, while the Federal Reserve’s latest interest rate hike adds to borrowing costs. Regionally, sales declined sharply in the South (-6.6% to 413,000) and the Midwest (-7.3% to 89,000), while rising in the West (21.4% to 142,000) and the Northeast (28.0% to 32,000). The supply of new homes for sale fell to 476,000, equivalent to 8.4 months of supply at the current sales pace. Meanwhile, the median sales price edged up to $400,400, its highest level in three months.

US Fifth District Manufacturing Remains in Contraction — The Federal Reserve’s Fifth District manufacturing index stood at -2 in October 2026, unchanged from September, signaling contraction for a second consecutive month. ### 2026-10-27 21:55:19  #市場 #國際

【德意志銀行：美股可能正處於年內最後一輪下跌的低點，年底上漲行情有望啟動】德意志銀行全球宏觀研究主管Jim Reid 10月27日表示，根據1928年以來標普500指數的歷史季節性表現，美股可能正處於年內最後一輪下跌的低點，隨後有望迎來年底上漲行情。歷史資料顯示，普通年份美股通常在9月初見頂、9月底觸底反彈，隨後於10月下旬再次回落，並從此逐步上漲至年底。美國中期選舉年份的規律更為明顯，標普500指數通常從4月底高點下跌約4%至5%，於10月初觸底；過去23箇中期選舉年份中，有21次指數在10月初之後的三個月內上漲。Reid指出，今年標普500指數5月29日創下歷史新高後，近五個月累計漲幅不足1%，整體走勢與歷史中期選舉年份較為接近。隨著11月3日中期選舉臨近，選舉週期與年底季節性上漲因素可能形成共振，但伊朗局勢、AI及法國政治風險仍可能擾亂市場，歷史規律也不意味著股市必然上漲。

US Home Price Growth Accelerates Further — The S&P Cotality Case-Shiller 20-City Home Price Index rose 2.7% year-on-year in August 2026, marking its strongest annual increase since May 2025 and exceeding market expectations of 2.5%. Chicago led all metropolitan areas for the sixth consecutive month, with prices rising 6.7%, followed by New York (6.2%) and Cleveland (4.3%). On the West Coast, Seattle recorded the steepest annual decline for the third consecutive month at 2.0%, followed by Las Vegas (-1.3%) and Denver (-0.8%). On a monthly basis, home prices fell 0.4% in August after remaining unchanged in July. However, inflation-adjusted prices rose 0.3%, suggesting underlying strength despite the typical post-summer slowdown. S&P Dow Jones Indices’ Rebecca Kaufman noted that affordability remains challenging, as elevated living costs, oil prices near $100 per barrel and mortgage rates in the mid-to-high 6% range continue to weigh on households.

Gasoline Futures Retreat — US gasoline futures fell below $3.20 per gallon after reaching a one-week high amid hopes of a diplomatic resolution to the conflict in the Middle East. Iranian leader Khamenei said officials should ensure conditions for ending the war are met, urging an end to the "neither war nor peace" state. The statement adds optimism to a persistently tight fuel market. EIA data showed US gasoline inventories fell by nearly 3 million barrels in the week ending October 16th, reversing previous builds and and leaving stocks below seasonal norms. Supplies are expected to stay constrained as refineries prioritize higher-demand diesel ahead of winter. Routine maintenance also limits gasoline output despite facilities running near capacity, with Morgan Stanley warning tight conditions could persist through mid-2027. Still, pump prices have eased in recent weeks as driving demand wavers. Geopolitical risks added to concerns, with Russia and Ukraine continuing to exchange heavy air strikes.

### 2026-10-27 20:37:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Durable Goods Orders #Importance 3

US Durable Goods Orders Rise More Than Expected — New orders for US-manufactured durable goods increased 1.8% month-on-month in September 2026, following an upwardly revised 0.1% gain in August and exceeding market expectations of a 1.5% rise. The increase was driven by a strong rebound in transportation equipment orders, which climbed 4.6% after falling 0.3% in August, led by a 28.4% surge in civilian aircraft bookings. Elsewhere, orders rose for machinery (1.2% vs. 1.0%), computers and electronic products (0.8% vs. 0.5%), primary metals (0.5% vs. 1.9%) and fabricated metal products (0.4% vs. -1.4%). Excluding transportation, new orders increased 0.5%, following a 0.3% gain in August, while orders excluding defense surged 2.2%, compared with a 0.3% rise in the previous month. Meanwhile, orders for non-defense capital goods excluding aircraft, a closely watched indicator of business investment plans, rose 0.5% after increasing 1.6% in August.

### 2026-10-27 20:29:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Balance of Trade #Importance 2

Mexico Trade Deficit Wider Than Expected — Mexico’s trade balance posted a deficit of $606 million in September 2026, compared with a $240 million deficit a year earlier and market expectations of a $400 million shortfall. Total merchandise exports rose 37.9% year over year to $77.80 billion, with non-oil shipments up 38.8% and oil exports up 9.6%. Within non-oil exports, shipments to the US increased 43.5%, while sales to other markets rose 16.4%. Manufactured goods sales increased 39%, led by electrical and electronic equipment (94.7%), mining and metallurgical products (15.5%), and machinery and equipment for various industries (14.7%). Imports climbed 33.1% to $78.40 billion, as intermediate goods purchases jumped 38.8%, capital goods imports rose 18.7%, and consumer goods imports increased 11.4%. From August, the trade balance swung from a $605 million surplus, as the non-oil surplus narrowed to $2.75 billion from $4.05 billion. In the first nine months of 2026, the trade balance recorded a $9.25 billion surplus.

【抖音李亮：騰訊公司短劇、短影片增長速度遠超抖音和紅果】抖音集團李亮發文稱，隨著紅果短劇內容質量不斷提升，越來越受到使用者喜愛。我之前推薦過《冒姓琅琊》，再推薦幾部短劇給大家：歷史劇《持械入宋》《魏家逆子成首輔》《大明，李景隆的別樣人生》；科普劇《急診摸魚王》《並肩》《向陽而生》。我們也同時發現，不斷有關於紅果短劇的不實資訊傳出，這些資訊甚至影響到一些有權威的專家學者，一位我非常尊重的學者居然提出“中國網際網路的位元組時間”，這個定性極不嚴謹，純屬瞎扯！網際網路競爭激烈，沒有哪家公司成為“中國網際網路的xx時間”。網際網路產品中，只有即時通訊因為有關係鏈這個護城河，市場格局相對穩定。短劇、短影片這類產品屬於充分競爭行業，市場競爭激烈，市場格局變動快，沒有哪家公司能穩居市場第一，真實情況是，騰訊公司短劇、短影片增長速度，遠遠超過抖音和紅果，而且在網際網路應用中，即時通訊，遊戲、電商、音樂都佔據使用者非常多的“時間”。這位學者提出所謂“中國網際網路的xx時間”，非常誇張，不嚴謹，不符合事實，也經不住時間的考驗，其實也不符合他本人真實的風格。

### 2026-10-27 20:17:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Producer Price Inflation MoM #Importance 2

Brazil Producer Prices Rise the Most in 5 Months — Brazil’s producer prices rose 1.54% month-on-month in September 2026, marking its highest level since April and accelerating sharply from a 0.34% rise in the prior month. Inflation in the mining and quarrying sector surged to 11.04%, its highest level since March, from 4.34% in the previous month. This reflected higher prices for crude petroleum oils, mined iron and iron concentrates. Price growth in manufacturing accelerated to 1.12% from 0.17% in August. Food price inflation increased to 2.4%, its highest level since September 2024, from 1.99%, while prices in the manufacture of coke, refined petroleum products and biofuels rebounded by 4.88% following a 0.16% decline in the previous month. On an annual basis, producer prices accelerated to 4.34% in September, its highest level since May 2025, up from 2.5% in August.

### 2026-10-27 20:13:56  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 2

Dollar Gains for 3rd Session — The dollar index strengthened to 102.2 on Tuesday, extending gains for a third consecutive session and reaching its highest level in about two weeks. Investors brace for a busy week of monetary policy decisions from major central banks, with the FOMC meeting kicking off today. The Federal Reserve is widely expected to leave the fed funds rate unchanged, although investors will look for clues on whether a rate hike in December remains a possibility. The European Central Bank, Bank of Japan and Bank of Canada are also due to announce their policy decisions this week. The greenback also drew support from improved stability in equity markets, as sentiment surrounding AI-related stocks steadied following last week’s volatility. Meanwhile, the dollar traded mostly higher against the euro, which remained under pressure from France’s deteriorating fiscal outlook and mounting concerns in the bond market.

### 2026-10-27 20:11:01  #國際

【AI競選廣告轟炸美中期選舉】隨著11月3日美國中期選舉臨近，大量由人工智慧（AI）生成的競選廣告出現在電視和社交媒體上。追蹤美國選舉的“衛斯理媒體專案”組織稱，已在全國識別出220個至少部分使用AI工具生成的政治廣告，總耗資超過1.37億美元，而2024年選舉週期此類廣告支出不足5萬美元。據不完全統計，這些廣告中81%由共和黨候選人或團體贊助，12%由民主黨贊助，且大部分未標註“AI生成”字樣。 民主黨籍聯邦參議員奧索夫本人就在AI廣告中被“黑”。他直言“看到深度偽造影片被用來捏造政治廣告，深感不安”。近期調查顯示，73%的受訪者認為競選活動不應使用AI生成的影象或影片。不過，研究人員認為，AI廣告對“選擇投給誰”影響目前來看相對較小，但可能在動員投票或籌款方面更有效。“衛斯理媒體專案”負責人福勒指出，美國聯邦層面目前對AI政治廣告沒有任何監管，競選團隊沒有義務標註，而未標註的AI廣告“特別危險”，建議公眾對資訊多留心眼、回源頭核實。（CCTV國際時訊）

【央視要求皇馬和姆巴佩書面道歉】針對西甲皇家馬德里隊球員姆巴佩嚴重涉華負面言論，中央廣播電視總檯於10月26日第一時間進行反制，叫停了西甲賽事以及涉及姆巴佩的所有比賽和其代言廣告的播出。總檯負責人同時致函西甲聯盟主席特巴斯，要求皇馬和姆巴佩本人立即公開誠懇道歉，消除不良影響，並向總檯遞交書面道歉信。北京時間10月27日下午，西甲聯盟主席哈維爾·特巴斯致函總檯負責人，對姆巴佩的行為予以譴責並表示道歉。特巴斯說：“我希望立即且明確地表達我們對球員基利安·姆巴佩行為的譴責。這是不可接受的行為，不代表我們賽事或者西班牙足球的價值觀。我們理解此事在中國引發的憤慨，並對造成的冒犯深表歉意。”特巴斯還表示，我謹親自向中國人民轉達我們的尊重，以及我們對多年來所建立關係的堅定承諾。我們正在採取適當措施，以最嚴肅的態度處理這一情況，並將隨時向各位通報所採取的行動。在收到特巴斯的信函後，中央廣播電視總檯快速做了回應：先賠禮道歉，否則一切免談。在以總檯臺長名義的回函中，總檯明確表示：西甲、皇家馬德里俱樂部，特別是姆巴佩本人必須儘快公開道歉，這是基本前提。（上觀新聞）

Brent Crude Eases as Diplomacy Picks Up — Brent crude fell below $115 a barrel on Tuesday, following a 0.8% loss on Monday, as renewed diplomatic efforts raised hopes of ending the US-Iran war and easing pressure on energy markets. Iran’s Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei called for an end to the current state of “neither war nor peace,” signaling a possible opening for negotiations. Officials from Saudi Arabia, Pakistan and Turkey met in Tehran on Monday and agreed to continue discussions, while Russian President Vladimir Putin was expected to meet Iran’s President Masoud Pezeshkian and Qatar’s emir in Moscow. These developments improved prospects for de-escalation, although significant risks remain. A Saudi-led coalition reported intercepting drones and ballistic missiles launched by Iran-backed Houthi rebels, while Hezbollah was reportedly preparing for renewed conflict in Lebanon. Investors are also monitoring upcoming elections, including in Israel, for potential implications for regional stability and oil supply.

Sensex Closes Almost Muted — India’s BSE Sensex closed virtually unchanged at 73,598.7 after a choppy session on Tuesday, amid continued pressure from elevated crude oil prices and foreign fund outflows, while caution prevailed ahead of the US Federal Reserve’s policy decision. Weakness in banking and information technology stocks was cushioned by gains in auto and pharma stocks following upbeat earnings from companies in both sectors.Among individual stocks, SBIN (-1.6%), Axis Bank (-1.5%), Asian Paints (-1.4%), HDFC Bank (-1.2%) and ICICI Bank (-1%) emerged as the biggest laggards. On the upside, Bharti Airtel climbed 3.2% to the top of the index, on expectations of tariff hikes and stronger earnings ahead of Jio Platforms’ listing, scheduled for October 28. Other top performers included Maruti (2.3%), which reported higher second-quarter revenue despite a decline in net profit. Trent, Power Grid, Eternal, BEL ,Tata Steel and Sun Pharma also advanced solidly, rising between 1%-2.4%.

【印度新增煉油產能明年投產 柴油出口將大增】隨著新煉油產能投產，印度柴油出口將在明年放量，為美國、歐洲等燃油緊缺市場送去亟需的供應。印度石油部長Hardeep Singh Puri週二表示，透過新建煉廠以及現有裝置擴建，國有煉油商將新增至少1500萬噸的年度柴油產能。他在果阿一場行業會議間隙對記者稱：“我們將迎來有利局面，不僅能滿足本國煉油需求，還可以供應全球。”這場市場動盪給印度帶來機遇。印度本就是全球主要柴油供應國之一，柴油是支撐農業、工廠與重型運輸的核心燃料。Kpler資料顯示，印度今年已超越俄羅斯，成為全球第二大柴油出口國。高盛預計全球柴油供應緊張局面將持續至2027年，本次煉廠擴建將進一步提升印度在全球燃油市場的份額。

【Reserve Bank of India deputy governor: Emerging markets must bolster domestic financing capacity】Reserve Bank of India deputy governor Punam Gupta said on Tuesday that external capital flows are highly unstable and emerging markets, including India, need to strengthen domestic financing capacity and build buffers to withstand volatile foreign flows. She urged countries to reduce macro-level reliance on external financing. Despite rapid Indian growth, foreign funding has become less reliable, increasing depreciation pressure on the rupee. Through the fiscal year to March, capital inflows weakened sharply, forcing India to draw on FX reserves to plug external financing gaps. Earlier this month the RBI unveiled a package of measures aimed at curbing market bets on excessive rupee depreciation.

### 2026-10-27 19:17:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Loan Growth #Importance 1

Greece Credit Growth Slows to Lowest in Over 2 Years — The annual growth of total credit extended to the Greek economy slowed to 2.4% in September 2026, its weakest pace since January 2024, down from 3.5% in August. Despite this deceleration, net lending flows turned positive, reaching €744 million after a €250 million outflow in the previous month. Credit growth to the private sector eased to 6% from 7.3%, although monthly net flows improved markedly to €735 million from €489 million. Meanwhile, credit to the general government contracted at a faster annual pace of 3.6%, compared with 2.7% in August. However, monthly net flows turned marginally positive at €9 million, following a €250 million decline in the previous month.

### 2026-10-27 19:17:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ireland #Producer Prices Change #Importance 0

Ireland Wholesale Inflation Highest Since 2022 — Wholesale prices in Ireland rose 7.4% year-on-year in September 2026, marking the highest level since October 2022, accelerating from a 6.5% increase in August. Domestic prices increased 2.4%, while export producer prices rose 8.1%. Producer prices recorded notable year-on-year increases in chemicals and chemical products (16.5%), other non-metallic mineral products (6.3%), electrical equipment (6.1%), fabricated metal products excluding machinery and equipment (5.5%), and basic metals (5.1%). Food products rose 1.2%, driven by fish and fish products (9.6%) and other food products (6.3%), while meat and meat products fell 9.4%. Meanwhile, energy fuels surged 47.6%, electricity prices jumped 92.8%, and construction products rose 3.8%. On a monthly basis, wholesale prices rose 0.5% in September, reversing a 0.8% decline in August.

【西甲聯盟致歉】西甲聯盟稱：對於基利安·姆巴佩近期言論給中國球迷帶來的冒犯與傷害，西甲聯盟深感遺憾。相關言論屬於球員個人表達，絕不代表西甲聯盟的立場。作為聯賽管理機構，在我們應承擔的責任範圍內，西甲聯盟謹代表聯盟及旗下所有俱樂部，向中國球迷致以誠摯的歉意。 西班牙足球始終秉持尊重、和諧共處的理念，致力於促進不同文化背景的人們團結相連。我們始終對中國、各相關機構及中國人民懷有深切的敬意，也無比珍視數百萬中國球迷每週給予我們的關注與支援。我們在此重申，將一如既往地珍惜並維護多年來與中國建立的深厚關係。 目前，西甲聯盟正與中國的合作伙伴及相關機構共同努力，妥善處理此次事件。此外，我們將攜手各俱樂部及球員，建立並推行文化認知與溝通規範，進一步促進對不同文化的理解與尊重，努力避免類似情況再次發生。 （北京青年報）

【Survey: BOC may hike as inflation-rebound risk rises】Most economists in a Wall Street Journal survey expect the BOC to raise rates on Wednesday, the bank's first hike in over three years. Governor MACKLEM has highlighted rising fuel costs that could push up non-energy goods prices, a key driver of market expectations for tightening. The survey and analysts report higher inflation expectations among firms and households, prompting a bias toward earlier action. Despite US trade-policy uncertainty, Canada’s economy remains relatively resilient with retail sales and exports beating forecasts. In the WSJ poll of 15 economists, 11 forecast a 25bp hike to a 2.50% overnight rate target and four expect no change; most still see at least one additional hike later despite some soft patches in the economy.

【伊朗最高領袖會見最高國家安全委員會秘書雷扎伊】當地時間27日，伊朗最高領袖穆傑塔巴會見伊朗最高國家安全委員會秘書雷扎伊。穆傑塔巴表示，制定一份基於對每項行動的全面路線圖，是做出合理穩健決策的先決條件。加強民族凝聚力和團結、增進人民與官員之間的互信；利用憲法，賦予總統和行政機構的權力，協助其促進和加快解決國家問題；透過對霍爾木茲海峽的有效管控來維護國家和地區安全；在可持續和共同利益的基礎上加強與鄰國的關係，是本次會議討論的議題。穆傑塔巴認為，敵人的絕望是伊朗抵抗的成果之一，並強調要積極及時地傳遞資訊，使人民繼續參與保衛國家的鬥爭。同時要透過落實國家官員提出的各項需求，維護伊朗及整個抵抗陣線的權利和利益，以期結束戰爭。會議上，雷扎伊就國家各領域的現狀和正在開展的工作進行了彙報。（央視新聞）

【BlackRock: Energy shock to leave long-term scars on European economy】BlackRock strategists said the US–Iran conflict-driven energy price surge will have a persistent negative impact on the European economy, while potentially benefiting energy infrastructure firms. They warned that buffers that absorbed the first-round shock earlier this year are fading and Europe is particularly vulnerable. Rising energy costs have knocked about 0.5% off euro-area GDP relative to a no-price-rise baseline. Strategists expect assets able to withstand future energy shocks—energy infrastructure—to attract increasing investor demand, and note that higher bond yields have depressed equities, leaving US and European utility stocks looking relatively attractive.

【深圳股交獲批私募股權投資和創業投資基金份額轉讓試點】中國證監會近日正式批覆，同意在深圳前海股權交易中心開展私募股權投資和創業投資基金份額轉讓試點，這是深圳區域性股權市場服務創新資本的關鍵突破。此次試點獲批，標誌著深圳股交具備了開展私募基金份額轉讓及相關配套服務的合規資質。深圳股交現已制定完成“1+N”業務規則體系，明確基金份額轉讓的掛牌條件、材料要求、交易方式及監管機制；同時，已上線業務系統，為私募基金份額交易提供綜合服務。深圳股交將進一步補齊深圳私募創投行業退出環節短板，打通“募投管退”良性迴圈通道，為創新資本“進得來、留得住、退得出”提供堅實的平臺支撐。

### 2026-10-27 18:23:16  #MKT News

【Malaysia palm oil oversupply strains storage in main producing region】Malaysia's palm oil stocks have climbed to record levels, pressuring storage capacity in key producing regions. Industry sources say output in Sarawak on Borneo has exceeded expectations and a surge in freight rates has slowed shipments, leaving local storage tanks nearing capacity. As the world's second-largest palm oil producer, Malaysia's September stocks rose to a record 3.51 mln t and monthly production hit a historic high, with the seasonal October peak not yet arrived. Rising inventories have increased pressure on the government to clear backlogs; officials are considering exempting crude palm oil export tax to boost overseas sales. The supply glut has weighed on prices: Kuala Lumpur benchmark futures fell as much as 2.2% on Tuesday, the largest drop in over a month and trading near a four-month low reached in early October. The price decline has widened palm oil's discount to competing vegetable oils, supporting this month's exports, but elevated stocks limit upside for a sustained rebound.

### 2026-10-27 18:23:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bosnia and Herzegovina #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Bosnia and Herzegovina Inflation at 5-Month High — The annual inflation rate in Bosnia and Herzegovina rose to 6.0% in September 2026, marking the highest level since April, from 4.9% in the previous month. Inflation accelerated for housing and utilities (12.7% vs 11.3% in August), transport (27.6% vs 22.9%), furnishings, household equipment, and routine maintenance (2.2% vs 1.9%), health (5.8% vs 5.5%), education (2.3% vs 1.8%), and other goods and services (2.0% vs 1.4%). Conversely, inflation eased for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (3.8% vs 3.9%), communication (1.7% vs 3.1%), recreation and culture (3.8% vs 3.9%), and restaurants and hotels (4.3% vs 4.8%). Meanwhile, deflation eased for food and non-alcoholic beverages (-0.1% vs -0.9%) and clothing and footwear (-6.9% vs -8.7%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.2% in September, following a 1.1% increase in August.

### 2026-10-27 18:15:37  #公司

【泡泡瑪特：公司實施庫存動態管理，減少不必要的商品召回】藍鯨新聞10月27日電，在今日泡泡瑪特三季度財報電話會上，公司管理層談及今年備貨情況時表示，Q4要持續降低整體庫存水位，同時保障新品供給。公司會重點關注頭程調撥的有效性，具體而言，一些國家和地區內部的商品網路具備較大的調撥空間，透過貨品調配（allocation），可以持續補充網路內的缺貨，提高銷售。公司已開始逐步實施動態管理，在初次配貨後持續調整，而不是等到終端售罄率達到較高水平，再開展後續調撥。這有助於減少不必要的商品召回，以及門店間高頻、反覆的調貨。（藍鯨新聞）

---

### 2026-10-27 18:15:29  #公司

【泡泡瑪特：公司仍以中國為核心生產基地】藍鯨新聞10月27日電，在今日泡泡瑪特三季度財報電話會上，公司管理層表示，目前，公司仍以中國為核心生產基地，國內生產基地正從廣東向內陸地區拓展，以降低部分生產成本。越南是公司嘗試海外生產的第一站，當地生產的效率和質量仍處於爬坡階段，基本達到公司要求。雖然越南生產具有一定成本優勢，但其面向歐美市場的物流時效優勢並不明顯，難以顯著縮短海外訂單交付週期。此外公司也在墨西哥進行了輕量嘗試，但認為墨西哥未來成為主要生產基地的可能性較低。目前解決海外訂單交付問題主要透過前置規劃、多次出貨和結合多種物流方式解決。（藍鯨新聞 ）

Elevated oil prices and geopolitical uncertainty added to the headwinds, increasing costs for energy-importing economies such as South Africa and complicating central banks’ policy decisions. ### 2026-10-27 17:18:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Unemployment Rate #Importance 2

Spain Unemployment Rate Back Above 10% — Spain’s unemployment rate rose to 10.18% in Q3 2026, up from 9.87% in Q2 and above market expectations of 9.9%. The number of unemployed people increased by 95,900, or 3.8%, from the previous quarter, marking the largest rise for a third quarter since Q3 2020. Nevertheless, the unemployment rate was the lowest recorded for a third quarter since 2007. The number of unemployed people reached 2.591 million. Meanwhile, employment increased by 73,600, or 0.3%, marking the weakest gain for a third quarter since 2019. Despite the modest growth, total employment reached a record high of 22.852 million. The number of employed foreign nationals rose by 75,400, or 2%, while employment among Spanish nationals declined by 1,800. By sector, public employment increased by 89,900, while private-sector employment fell by 16,300. The labour force expanded by 169,500 to a record 25.443 million, pushing the participation rate up to 59.5%.

### 2026-10-27 16:54:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #Money Supply M2 #Importance 0

Taiwan M2 Money Supply Hits Record High — Taiwan’s M2 money supply rose 7.24% year-on-year to a record TWD 71,361 billion in September 2026, accelerating from 6.79% in the previous month. The faster expansion reflected higher annual growth in lending and investment, as well as the temporary repatriation of overseas financing funds by enterprises. On a monthly basis, M2 increased 0.97%, while average growth for the first nine months of the year stood at 6.69%. Meanwhile, lending and investment by all monetary institutions grew 9.79% year-on-year, up from 9.43% in August, driven primarily by increased claims on the private sector, public enterprises, and government debt. Including lending and investment by life insurers and adjusting for bad-debt write-offs transferred by monetary institutions, the broader measure rose 8.06%, accelerating from 7.69% in the previous month.

Zinc Rises to 4-Week High — Zinc futures rose to $3,830 per tonne, the highest level in four weeks, supported by declining inventories, high electricity costs and tight near-term supply. Stocks held on the Shanghai Futures Exchange dropped 5% from the previous week to 137,139 tonnes, marking their lowest level since March, while inventories on the London Metal Exchange stood at 133,700 tonnes. Supply pressures have been particularly pronounced in Western markets, where zinc output declined 3.4% year-on-year in the first half of 2026, weighed down by elevated energy prices and limited raw material availability. Uncertainty over the future of Nyrstar’s loss-making Budel smelter in the Netherlands has also raised some concerns over the supply outlook. Meanwhile, China’s refined zinc output fell 1.9% month-on-month and 6.4% year-on-year in September. However, the prospect of a recovery in Chinese mine production and improved concentrate availability could ease supply tightness and cap further price gains.

### 2026-10-27 15:55:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Consumer Confidence #Importance 2

French Consumer Morale Lowest Since 2013 — The consumer confidence indicator in France fell to 80 in October 2026, below expectations and September’s 85, reaching its lowest level since June 2013. The decline comes amid rising French bond yields, fiscal uncertainty over austerity measures and protests over the cost of living. Consumers became more pessimistic about their expected (-24 vs -15 in September) and their past (-32 vs -26) financial situation. Major purchase sentiment weakened to -41 from -37, while the opportunity to save declined to 37 from 43 and expected saving capacity fell to 12 from 15. Meanwhile, unemployment expectations rose to 61 from 56, while sentiment toward France’s future standard of living deteriorated to -67 from -61. The assessment of past living standards fell to -85 from -82, its lowest level since the survey began in October 1972. The savings climate index declined to 118 from 120. Perceptions of past price increases rose to 42 from 34, while inflation expectations improved to -7 from -9.

### 2026-10-27 15:48:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Iron Ore Languishes at Four-Year Lows — Iron ore futures declined toward CNY 660 per ton on Tuesday, hitting fresh four-year lows amid signs of abundant global supply and a deteriorating demand outlook. China is entering a seasonally weaker period for steel consumption, while elevated oil prices have heightened concerns about the broader global economic outlook. Deteriorating profitability among Chinese steelmakers has further weighed on demand expectations, with industry data showing that the blast furnace operating rate across 247 Chinese steel mills stood at 80% last week, down 4.3 percentage points from the same period a year earlier. On the supply side, shipments from four major iron ore producers, including Rio Tinto Group and BHP Group, are expected to increase by nearly 20 million tons in the fourth quarter, while Guinea’s Simandou mine continues to expand production.

### 2026-10-27 15:37:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brunei #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Brunei Consumer Prices Fall for First Time Since March — Brunei’s consumer prices edged down 0.1% year-on-year in September 2026, reversing a 0.5% increase in the previous month and marking the first decline in six months. Prices fell for clothing (-6.1% vs 2.4% in August), communication (-3.5% vs -3.5%), recreation (-0.7% vs 0.7%), and miscellaneous goods and services (-0.1% vs 0.3%). Transport prices were muted at 0.02%, after a 0.6% increase in August. Housing costs remained nearly flat (at 0.02%). Meanwhile, inflation eased for food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.3% vs 1.7%) and restaurants and hotels (0.5% vs 0.7%), while education inflation held steady (at 0.3%). In contrast, prices accelerated for furnishings, household equipment and routine maintenance (0.9% vs 0.3%) and health (0.8% vs 0.6%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell 0.2%, swinging from a 0.3% increase in August and signaling the fifth monthly decline so far this year.

### 2026-10-27 15:35:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lithuania #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Lithuania Retail Sales Growth Rises in September — Retail sales in Lithuania rose by 5.6% year-on-year in September, following a revised 3.5% increase in the previous month. Growth accelerated in retail trade, excluding automotive fuel (7.7% vs. 5.5% in August), non-food items (11.9% vs. 8.8%), textiles and clothing (21.2% vs. 9.8%), audio, video, hardware, furniture and lighting (17.1% vs. 13.9%), and pharmaceutical, medical and cosmetic products (11.6% vs. 9.3%). Sales also increased for food, beverages and tobacco (1.4% vs. 0.8%), information and communication technology equipment, cultural and recreational goods, watches, jewelry and other new goods (2.5% vs. 1.8%), and mail-order and online sales (6.2% vs. 3.6%). Meanwhile, automotive fuel sales declined at a slower pace (-1.7% vs. -3.3%). On a monthly basis, retail sales edged up 0.5% in September, rebounding from a revised 0.1% decline in August.

### 2026-10-27 15:20:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Loan Growth #Importance 1

Swedish Household Lending Growth at Near 4-Year High — Swedish household lending growth edged up to 3.4% year-on-year in September 2026 from 3.3% in the prior month, marking its highest rate since December 2022. Housing loans, which accounted for 83% of household credit, grew 3.6%, while consumer loans, representing 6%, increased 1.6%. Meanwhile, lending to non-financial corporations expanded 4.0%. Borrowing costs were mixed. The average interest rate on new housing loan agreements rose to 2.83% from 2.78%, while the average floating mortgage rate edged down to 2.72% from 2.73%. For housing loans with maturities of over one to five years, the average fixed rate increased to 3.39% from 3.18% in August. The average fixed rate on new agreements with maturities of over three months to one year also climbed to 3.04% from 2.79%. Meanwhile, household deposits edged up to SEK 2,974 billion from SEK 2,970 billion, with demand deposits accounting for 74% of total savings, or SEK 2,200 billion.

### 2026-10-27 15:18:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Sweden Trade Surplus Widens in September — Sweden recorded a trade surplus of SEK 4.1 billion in September 2026, widening from SEK 3.4 billion in the same month a year earlier, with exports and imports rising at the same pace. Exports grew 18.7% year-on-year to a record SEK 206.4 billion, supported by increased sales to EU markets (+19.2% to SEK 116.0 billion) and non-EU markets (+18.0% to SEK 90.4 billion). Imports also rose 18.7% to an all-time high of SEK 202.3 billion, driven by purchases from EU countries (+19.2% to SEK 137.4 billion) and non-EU countries (+17.6% to SEK 64.9 billion). For the first nine months of the year, the country posted a trade deficit of SEK 0.8 billion, shifting from a surplus of SEK 42.4 billion in the previous year, as imports increased 8.6%, outpacing the 5.5% rise in exports. Seasonally adjusted, net trade showed a surplus of SEK 0.5 billion in September, down slightly from SEK 0.6 billion in August.

Thailand Car Sales Rise 14.28% in September — Thailand’s domestic vehicle sales rose 14.28% year-on-year to a five-month low of 55,254 units in September 2026, easing from 25.59% in the previous month, according to data from the Federation of Thai Industries (FTI). Still, it marked the seventh straight month of growth, highlighting the continued recovery in domestic demand. Meanwhile, vehicle production declined 6.08% year-on-year to 120,319 units, reversing a 10.93% increase in August. For the full year, car production is expected to reach 1.45 million units in 2026. Thailand, Southeast Asia’s largest automotive production hub, also recorded a 4.55% increase in vehicle exports, rebounding from a 2.04% decline in the previous month.

### 2026-10-27 15:14:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #Lithuania #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Lithuania Industrial Output Accelerates — Industrial production in Lithuania rose 7.4% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating from a downwardly revised 4.6% increase in the previous month. Growth picked up in manufacturing, reaching 8.0% from 5.2% in August, and in mining and quarrying, rising to 11.8% from 9.3%. The pace of decline also eased in electricity, gas, steam, and air conditioning supply, where output fell 5.6% after an 8.1% drop in August. Meanwhile, the downturn continued in water supply, sewerage, waste management, and remediation services, with output falling 10.1%, compared with a 10.0% contraction in the previous month. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial production rose 1.8%, after remaining flat in August.

### 2026-10-27 15:12:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Leading Economic Index #Importance 1

South Africa Leading Index Hits 5-Month High — South Africa’s composite leading business cycle indicator rose 0.9% month-on-month in August 2026, rebounding from a downwardly revised 0.8% fall in the previous month. It marked the highest reading since March, as six of the ten available component time series increased. The largest positive contributions came from an acceleration in the six-month smoothed growth rate of real M1 money supply and an increase in the number of residential building plans approved. Conversely, the largest negative contributions came from decelerations in the six-month smoothed growth rates of job advertisements and new passenger vehicle sales. Meanwhile, the composite coincident business cycle indicator rose 0.6% in July, supported by increases in the real value of wholesale, retail and motor trade sales, as well as in industrial production. However, the composite lagging business cycle indicator fell 0.4% in the same month.

### 2026-10-27 15:09:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Turkish Jobless Rate Highest in 7 Months — Turkey’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate rose to 8.4% in September 2026 from an upwardly revised 7.9% in the previous month. This was the highest jobless rate since February, as the number of unemployed individuals increased by 198,000 to 2.97 million. By gender, the jobless rate stood at 6.9% for men and 11.3% for women. Meanwhile, the employment rate edged down to 48.3% from 48.4%, as the number of employed persons fell by 49,000 to 32.45 million. The labor force participation rate inched up to 52.7% from 52.6% in the previous month, with the labor force increasing by 149,000 to 35.41 million. Among youth aged 15–24, the unemployment rate increased to 14.8% from 13.2%, with rates of 10.4% for men and 23.3% for women. Average weekly actual working hours stood at 42.4 hours in September, down by 0.1 hours from the previous month.

### 2026-10-27 15:04:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kosovo #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Kosovo Trade Deficit Widens in September — Kosovo’s trade deficit widened to €534.0 million in September 2026 from €520.5 million in the same month a year earlier. Imports rose 6.1% year-on-year to €643.4 million, driven by a 12.7% increase in purchases from EU countries, particularly Germany, which accounted for 12.5% of total imports, followed by Greece (6.5%) and Italy (4.4%). In contrast, imports from CEFTA countries declined by 1.2%. By product category, imports increased for mineral products (19.8%), beverages and tobacco (11.8%), and transport equipment (11.2%). Meanwhile, exports jumped 27.5% to €109.4 million, driven largely by a 22.1% increase in shipments to EU countries, particularly Germany, which accounted for 9.8% of total exports, followed by the Netherlands (2.7%) and Bulgaria (2.3%). Shipments to CEFTA countries also rose by 36.8%. By product category, exports increased for base metals and articles thereof (24.3%), mineral products (17.1%), and plastic and rubber products (10.3%).

### 2026-10-27 15:04:03  #國際

【中國作為首批簽約國簽署《聯合國可轉讓貨物單證公約》】當地時間10月26日，《聯合國可轉讓貨物單證公約》（以下簡稱《公約》）簽約儀式在迦納首都阿克拉舉行，共有60餘個國家和地區代表團約400人參加。商務部副部長蔣成華出席簽約儀式，並代表中國政府簽署《公約》。包括中國、迦納在內的26個國家成為《公約》的首批簽約國。《公約》此後將在紐約聯合國總部開放供各國簽署。

《公約》由中國發起並引領達成，經第80屆聯合國大會於2025年12月審議透過。《公約》透過在國際規則層面創設適用於所有跨境運輸方式的“可轉讓貨物單證”，打破數百年來只有海運提單才能代表貨權的制度壁壘。該制度創新填補了國際規則空白，為跨境運輸單證的融資交易提供國際法治保障，將有效緩解企業參與國際貿易的資金壓力，降低跨境陸上貿易交易成本，為全球貿易高質量發展注入新動能。《公約》的誕生是具有里程碑意義的國際商事規則創新，是中國和世界各國一道秉持推動構建人類命運共同體理念，深入踐行四大全球倡議的實際行動，也是堅持真正的多邊主義，維護以聯合國為核心的國際體系的生動寫照。

中方願與各國一道，儘快完成國內批准程式，也期待更多國家加入《公約》，使《公約》紅利惠及更多國家，為全球貿易繁榮、各國人民福祉作出貢獻。

### 2026-10-27 15:02:13  #國際

【不確定性抬升，英國股市成資金避風港】多重利空長期壓制英國股市：脫歐後資本投資疲軟、通脹預期偏高、AI賽道標的稀缺，疊加財政與債務擔憂，持續拖累市場。不過機構認為，現階段不宜看空英股。在G10經濟體中，英國股市自由現金流估值吸引力突出，金融股相對歐洲同業的折價創下30年高位。英國10年期名義收益率偏高主要反映通脹預期，而非債務信用風險。值得注意的是，英股科技敞口極低，科技板塊利潤佔比不足1%。在全球AI相關科技股波動加大、市場質疑大額AI基建投資回報的背景下，英股天然成為科技多頭的對沖選擇。歷史資料顯示，科技板塊走弱階段，英國市場往往有較好表現。不少英國消費、工業龍頭盈利韌性強、海外佈局多元，現金流回報優於歐洲同行，但已失去往日估值溢價。近期銀行、食品零售、電信等本土板塊盈利預期上調，疊加IMF上調英國增長預測，基本面邊際回暖。整體來看，倫敦股市規模萎縮、宏觀壓力仍存，但悲觀預期已較多反映在股價中，當前存在佈局高韌性價值標的的機會。

TAIEX Ends Higher but Holds Near 6-Week Low — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, climbed 55 points, or 0.1%, to close at 46,096 on Tuesday, paring gains from early trading and halting losses from the previous session as trading resumed after a two-day market closure. The broader index moved away from its lowest level since September 16, touched last Thursday, but remained close to that level as the heavyweight electronic technology sector was nearly flat. The slight gain tracked advances on Wall Street overnight amid a rebound in AI-related stocks. Moderating oil prices also boosted sentiment, easing concerns about inflation risks and interest rate hikes, although gains were limited by traders’ caution ahead of the Fed’s monetary policy decision this week. The technology services and financial sectors mainly lifted the index, with top performers including Nanya Technology Corp. (6.5%), Wiwiynn Corp (6.0%), Yageo Corp. (2.8%), Fubon Financial (2.2%), TSMC (0.6%), and Cathay Financial (0.4%).

【報告：逾九成受訪博士生每週數次或每天使用AI開展科研工作】10月26日，在中國科學技術協會、浙江省人民政府、世界青年科學家聯合會共同主辦的2026世界青年科學家峰會上，《科研之信：未來研究者》青年版報告正式釋出。報告顯示，AI已成為受訪博士生科研工作中經常呼叫的輔助資源，93.8%的受訪博士生每週數次或每天使用AI開展科研工作，其中67.5%已形成每日使用習慣。具體而言，ChatGPT的使用比例達到84.0%，被提及的其他工具包括Gemini（26.8%）、Claude（17.5%）和DeepSeek（11.3%），均為通用型AI工具。在具體的科研場景中，AI已逐步融入博士生科研工作的多個關鍵環節。報告顯示，78.4%的受訪博士已利用AI發現並總結最新研究進展，77.8%使用AI輔助文獻綜述，74.2%藉助AI分析科研資料；此外，95.9%的受訪博士生認可AI對科研的重要作用，而對AI工具總體可信度持積極評價的比例為59.8%，另有35.1%持中性評價、5.2%持否定評價。

### 2026-10-27 14:37:15  #市場 #國際

【韓交所主機板：外資超額收益是散戶六倍】在韓國綜合指數（KOSPI）主機板市場，外資採取追漲策略買入上漲個股，獲得的超額收益，是散戶逢下跌抄底買入所獲超額收益的六倍。散戶把股價下跌當作撿便宜的機會，而外資與機構資金傾向佈局已經處於上漲趨勢的股票，二者收益差距由此拉大。韓國資本市場研究院26日釋出一份韓國股市交易行為與股價表現研究報告：2020至2025年間，散戶淨買入佔比高的KOSPI個股，在買入之後的六個月裡，平均跑贏大盤僅0.21個百分點。與之對比，外資重點淨買入的個股，同期跑贏指數1.29個百分點，收益水平是散戶的六倍以上；機構淨買入個股跑贏指數0.82個百分點，同樣優於散戶。韓國資本市場研究院高階研究員Hong Ji-yeon表示：散戶淨買入組合沒有穩定超額收益，但機構與外資持倉能夠帶來相對穩健的超額回報。

【高盛：下調中信證券AH股目標價，投資收益拖累第三季盈利】高盛發表研報指，中信證券今年第三季淨收入及純利分別為169億元及79億元，同比分別跌27%及21%，環比跌36%及41%，較該行預期低29%及28%。盈利遜預期主要受投資收益拖累，投資收益37億元，同比跌71%、環比跌76%，該行預期低71%；投行收入及淨利息收入則分別較該行預期高28%及106%，提供部分抵銷。該行指，投資表現較弱歸因於股市偏軟、對人工智慧相關投資採取相對保守的估值，以及第二季比較基數偏高。高盛下調中信證券2026至2028年純利預測11%、2%及3%，至分別404億、407億及420億元，主要反映較低投資收益，部分被較高淨利息收入及較低經營開支抵銷。該行維持中信證券H股“中性”評級，目標價下調7%至31.27港元，對應預測2027年市盈率11倍；A股目標價由44.72元下調至41.72元，對應預測2027年市盈率16倍，評級“買入”。

The BOJ said September consumer prices excluding fresh food and special factors rose 3.2% YoY, up from 2.6% in August. Earlier Tuesday data showed service producer prices accelerated, partly linked to the Middle East conflict.

Finland Industrial Confidence Dips to 7-Month Low — Finland’s industrial confidence indicator edged down to 2 in October 2026 from 3 in the previous month, which was the lowest reading in five months. The latest figure marked the lowest level since March but remained above its long-term average of 0. Assessments of stocks of finished products fell to 3 from 5, while assessments of book orders deteriorated further, declining to -9 from -6 in September. Meanwhile, production expectations for the coming months remained unchanged at 19. In contrast, capacity utilization improved, with 67% of companies reporting that they were operating at full capacity, up from 66%. The survey also showed that Finnish industrial confidence remained significantly above the EU average, based on comparative data for September.

【New rule: cable TV barred from playing startup ads】Oct 27, 2026 — China’s National Radio and Television Administration issued the Regulations on Cable Radio and Television Operation Services, effective Nov. 27. The rules prohibit any organization or individual from altering cable broadcast content or intercepting, interfering with or descrambling transmission signals. Article 12 mandates a live-first principle: cable operators must provide a full-screen live-on-startup function, include a channel replay entry and may not play startup (power-on) advertising. Smart TVs must support priority live-channel display and offer an easy access settings entry.

ASX 200 Reverses Early Weakness to Close in the Green — The ASX 200 rose 40 points, or 0.5%, to end at 8,850 on Tuesday, turning early losses and extending gains for a third session. Easing oil prices helped temper inflation concerns ahead of Wednesday’s September CPI release, following August’s slowdown to 4.0%, below the 4.1% forecast but still above the RBA’s 2–3% target. Investors also awaited Governor Michele Bullock’s and other senior officials’ appearances before lawmakers, after Assistant Governor Sarah Hunter said the central bank had “not quite yet” done enough to curb inflation. Meanwhile, futures linked to the Dow Jones strengthened ahead of the Fed’s two-day policy meeting, which begins later today, with a rate decision due Wednesday. While a hold is widely expected, rate-hike expectations for December have increased. Gains in copper and iron ore lifted miners, including BHP Group (3.5%), Rio Tinto (2.5%), and Mineral Resources (2.4%). ### 2026-10-27 14:03:49  #央行 #市場 #國際

【只有美伊衝突結束黃金才能復甦？美銀策略師強調關鍵利空仍未解決】儘管分析師一直強調黃金的長期投資價值不變，但該資產的價格從年初的5600美元/盎司一路下跌，並在近期跌破4000美元/盎司，反映出市場對黃金越來越謹慎的態度。美國銀行財富管理高階投資策略師Rob Haworth指出，美伊衝突必須結束才能使黃金價格回到每盎司5000美元。他強調，儘管黃金的長期投資吸引力依然存在，但由於各國央行優先考慮經濟穩定和能源安全，而不是積累更多黃金，這種貴金屬繼續面臨負面壓力。只要美伊衝突繼續，黃金市場的壓力就會一直存在。他還補充稱，美伊衝突導致全球資本流動發生重大轉變，因為面臨能源和食品成本上漲的國家將資源從儲備多元化轉向其他用途。與此同時，債券收益率上升、美元走強以及流動性需求不斷增長，正在削弱黃金傳統的避險吸引力。

【聯儲證券首席經濟學家李全：中國資本市場有望步入發展新階段】聯儲證券董事、首席經濟學家李全在接受採訪時表示，資本市場改革已步入深水區，註冊制全面推進，多層次資本市場體系成為重要推動力，隨著產業結構最佳化、盈利能力釋放，中國資本市場有望步入發展新階段。在他看來，目前經濟環境與市場發展可以從兩個方面來把握。一方面，全球地緣衝突擾動、主要發達國家通脹高企、相繼加息，全球資本市場承壓，我國以新質生產力推動產業轉型、金融改革賦能實體、六網建設撬動投資，經濟穩中向好、韌性顯現；另一方面，全球處新一輪康波週期上升期，我國以科技信貸、科技保險、科技投資賦能科創，科創板、北交所深度助力科創企業資本化，為新一輪科技革命提供資本助力。 (財聯社)

Rupiah Holds Above IDR 18,100 Amid Mounting Pressures — The Indonesian rupiah remained above IDR 18,100 per U.S. dollar on Tuesday, hovering near its weakest level since late July as the dollar stayed firm ahead of the Fed’s upcoming policy decision. Locally, fiscal concerns added to pressure amid elevated oil prices and the government’s proposal to make education free from primary school through university, potentially adding to budget strains from free school meals and other costly commitments. Inflation risks also mounted, as September figures accelerated to a three-month high of 3.28%, reflecting rising food prices linked to El Niño. Meanwhile, Bank Indonesia Governor Destry Damayanti said the central bank would maintain its intervention strategy despite heightened volatility amid escalating Middle East tensions. She added that BI stayed active in the market, describing recent currency movements as part of normal market dynamics. A central bank official also said rupiah fluctuations remained manageable and within BI’s tolerance limits.

NZX 50 Drops for 3rd Straight Session, Ends 0.4% Lower — The NZX 50 fell 54 points, or 0.4%, to close at 13,680 on Tuesday, extending losses for a third straight session after trading resumed following the market’s closure on Monday for Labour Day. Traders were cautious ahead of the RBNZ’s and the Fed’s monetary policy decisions on Wednesday. The market expects the RBNZ to hike interest rates by 25 bps to 3%, following rate increases in July and September, amid high inflation driven by higher oil prices. Although annual inflation slowed to 3.7% in Q3, it remained above the RBNZ’s 1–3% target range. Caution persisted amid expectations that inflation would rise again following a temporary dip in fuel prices. However, lower oil prices limited the decline, easing inflation concerns and reducing expectations of an interest rate hike. The healthcare and consumer staples dragged down the index, with notable losses in Summerset Group (-4.7%), Seeka (-2.7%), Freightways Group (-2.0%), EBOS Group (-1.9%), A2 Milk (-1.7%), and Fisher & Paykel (-1.5%).

【Public voting begins in Israel's 26th Knesset election】Public voting in Israel's 26th Knesset election began Oct 27 at 07:00 local time (13:00 Beijing). The Israeli Central Elections Committee reported about 7.3 mln eligible voters and more than 12,000 polling stations; polling is open for a single day from 07:00 to 22:00. Official results are scheduled for Nov 4. Israel uses proportional representation: 120 seats allocated by vote share, a 3.25% threshold to enter the Knesset, and 61 seats required to form a government. No single party has ever won an outright majority; coalition government is the norm.

【崔東樹：2026年1-9月汽車行業利潤率3.3%、收入增2.6%、成本增3.8%、利潤降23%】崔東樹發文稱，2026年1-9月汽車生產2337萬臺，同比降4%，收入80435億元增2.6%，成本71627億元增3.8%，利潤2675億元降23%，銷售利潤率3.3%；其中9月的汽車生產306萬臺，銷售收入10374億元增0.6%，成本9257億元增2.5%，利潤142億元降68%，銷售利潤率1.4%，處於近4年的低位。2026年各地大力度推動“兩新”政策落地實施，逐步有效釋放內需活力，但汽車行業效益改善明顯落後其它消費品。隨著國家反內卷工作持續推進，汽車行業受上游擠壓嚴重，價格問題嚴重，油價暴漲，有色金屬、半導體利潤暴增，終端使用者購車觀望心態嚴重，車企執行壓力持續加大，高質量發展受到上游的巨大沖擊。從歷年銷售利潤率走勢來看，2024年汽車行業利潤表現已顯疲軟，銷售利潤率僅4.3%，較歷史正常水平大幅下降；2025年行業銷售利潤率降至4.1%。2026年1-9月行業銷售利潤率進一步降至3.3%，9月1.4%，低於2025年12月1.8%的最低月度表現。歷年9月一般是利潤率較低的時候，今年9月燃油車產銷壓力大，加之中秋因素，因此盈利壓力仍巨大。
